הנודר בשמו – a vow of abstinence (i.e., in place of a sacrifice and for consecration of an object – see Mishnah Nedarim, Chapter 1, Mishnah 2) from all the fruits of the world in the name of the such-and-such idol worship.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:6:8)


**הַמְקַיֵּם.** נִשְׁבָּע:


המקיים – swears


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:6:9)


**עוֹבֵר בְּלֹא תַעֲשֶׂה.** וְשֵׁם אֱלֹהִים אֲחֵרִים לֹא תַזְכִּירוּ (שם כג):


עובר בלא תעשה – (Exodus 23:13): “Make no mention of the names of other gods: [they shall not be heard on your lips].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:6:10)


**הַפּוֹעֵר עַצְמוֹ.** מַתְרִיז רְעִי לְפָנָיו:


הפוער עצמו – to splash excrement before it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:6:11)


**זוֹ הִיא עֲבוֹדָתוֹ.** וַאֲפִלּוּ נִתְכַּוֵּן לְבַזּוֹתוֹ, הוֹאִיל וְזוֹ הִיא עֲבוֹדָתוֹ חַיָּב חַטָּאת:


וזו היא עבודתו – and even if he intended to despise it, for since this is its worship, he is liable for a sin-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:6:12)


**וְהַזּוֹרֵק אֶבֶן לְמַרְקוּלִיס.** שֶׁעוֹבְדִים אוֹתוֹ בִּזְרִיקַת אֲבָנִים. וְהַמְסַלֵּק אֶבֶן מִלְּפָנָיו נַמִּי חַיָּב, שֶׁעוֹבְדִים אוֹתוֹ נַמִּי בְּסִלּוּק אֲבָנִים:


והזורק אבל למרקוליס – that we worship him with the throwing of stones, and the removing a stone from before him is also liable, for we also worship him by the removal of stones.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:6:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:6:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:6:13)


**מַרְקוּלִיס.** חִלּוּף הַשֶּׁבַח. מַר לְשׁוֹן חִלּוּף הוּא, כְּמוֹ בְּמַר דְּכַנְתָּא, בְּמַר דִּשְׁחוּטָה. קִלּוּס, שֶׁבַח:


מרקוליס – the reverse of praise; “bitter” is the language of reversal, such as: Master of the charm formula, or instead of the ritually slaughtered hen, praise/decry, praise/disgrace.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:6:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:6:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:6:14)


**זוֹ הִיא עֲבוֹדָתוֹ.** וַאֲפִלּוּ נִתְכַּוֵּן לְרָגְמוֹ, חַיָּב חַטָּאת:


זו היא עבודתו – and even if he intended to stone him, he is liable for a sin-offering.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:7:1)


**שֶׁיִּמְסֹר לַמֹּלֶךְ.** מוֹסְרוֹ בְּיַד הַכְּמָרִים:


שימסור למולך – transmit it in the hands of the [idolatrous] priests.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:7:2)


**וְיַעֲבִיר בָּאֵשׁ.** מַעֲבִירוֹ מִצַּד זֶה לְצַד זֶה:


ויעביר באש – pass him from one side to the other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:7:3)


**עַד שֶׁיִּמְסֹר וְיַעֲבִיר בָּאֵשׁ.** שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (ויקרא יח) לֹא תִתֵּן לְהַעֲבִיר, וּכְתִיב הָתָם (דברים יח) לֹא יִמָּצֵא בְךָ מַעֲבִיר בְּנוֹ וּבִתּוֹ בָּאֵשׁ, מַה הַעֲבָרָה הָאֲמוּרָה שָׁם בָּאֵשׁ, אַף הַעֲבָרָה הָאֲמוּרָה כָאן בָּאֵשׁ:


עד שימסור ויעביר באש – as it says (Leviticus 18:21): “[Do not allow any of your offspring] to be offered up [to Molech, and do not profane the name of your God: I am the LORD],” and it is written there (Deuteronomy 18:10): “Let no one be found among you who consigns his son or daughter to the fire…” Just as consigning stated there is by fire, so to the consigning mentioned here is by fire.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:7:4)


**אוֹב זֶה פִתּוֹם.** לוֹקֵחַ גֻּלְגֹּלֶת שֶׁל מֵת לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּתְאַכֵּל הַבָּשָׂר וּמַקְטִיר לָהּ וְשׁוֹאֵל מִמֶּנָּה עֲתִידוֹת וְהִיא מְשִׁיבָה:


אוב זה פיתום – to take the skull of the dead after the flesh had been consumed and make an idolatrous offering of incense with it and divine from it future events and it responds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:7:5)


**וְהַמְדַבֵּר מִשֶּׁחְיוֹ.** וְיֵשׁ שֶׁעוֹשִׂים שֶׁיָּשִׁיב הַמֵּת דֶּרֶךְ בֵּית הַשֶּׁחִי:


והמדבר משחיו – there are those who do it whereby the dead will respond via the arm-pit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:7:6)


**וְיִדְּעוֹנִי.** חַיָּה שֶׁשְּׁמָהּ יַדּוּעַ, וְצוּרָתָהּ כְּצוּרַת אָדָם בְּפָנֶיהָ וְיָדֶיהָ וְרַגְלֶיהָ, וְהִיא מְחֻבֶּרֶת בְּטַבּוּרָהּ לְחֶבֶל הַיּוֹצֵא מִן הַשֹּׁרֶשׁ שֶׁנִּשְׁרַשׁ בָּאָרֶץ שֶׁמִּשָּׁם חִיּוּתָהּ, וּכְשֶׁרוֹצִים לָצוּד אוֹתָהּ מוֹרִים בְּחִצִּים אֶל הַחֶבֶל עַד שֶׁהוּא נִפְסָק וְהִיא מֵתָה מִיָּד. וְהִיא קְרוּיָה בִלְשׁוֹן חֲכָמִים בַּר נָשׁ דְּטוּר:


ידעוני – a creature whose name is known and its form is like that of a human being in its face, hands and feet, and it is attached at its navel to a cord that goes out from the root that is rooted in the ground and from there is its life, and when we wish to hunt it, we shoot arrows to the cord until it is broken and it dies immediately. And it is called in the language of the Sages, “a human being of the mountain” [for divination].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:7:7)


**וְהַנִּשְׁאָל בָּהֶם.** שֶׁבָּא וְשׁוֹאֵל בָּהֶם לְהַגִּיד לוֹ דְבַר הֶעָתִיד [כְּגוֹן שָׁאוּל]:


והנשאל בהם – who comes and ask to know through them and to tell him something of the future such as Saul (see I Samuel, chapter 28, where Saul consults with the witch of Endor).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:7:8)


**בְּאַזְהָרָה.** דְּאַל תִּפְנוּ אֶל הָאֹבֹת:


באזהרה – (Leviticus 19:31): “Do not turn to ghosts [and do not inquire of familiar spirits, to be defiled by them: I the LORD am your God].”



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:8:1)


**עַד שֶׁיְּקַלְּלֵם בַּשֵּׁם.** בְּאֶחָד מִן הַשֵּׁמוֹת הַמְיֻחָדִים:


עד שיקללם בשם – with one of the special names.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:8:2)


**קִלְּלָם בְּכִנּוּי.** רַחוּם, חַנּוּן, אֶרֶךְ אַפַּיִם:


קללם בכינוי – “merciful,” “gracious,” “slow to anger” (see Exodus 34:7).



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:9:1)


**עַד שֶׁתְּהֵא נַעֲרָה.** וְלֹא קְטַנָּה פְּחוּתָה מִבַּת שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה וְיוֹם אֶחָד, וְלֹא בוֹגֶרֶת שֶׁעָבְרוּ עָלֶיהָ שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה וְשִׁשָּׁה חֳדָשִׁים וְיוֹם אֶחָד:


עד שתהא נערה – and not a female minor younger than the age of twelve years and one day, nor an adult woman who has passed twelve years, sixth months and a day.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:9:2)


**בְּתוּלָה.** וְלֹא בְעוּלָה. וְאִם נִבְעֲלָה שֶׁלֹּא כְדַרְכָּהּ עֲדַיִן בְּתוּלָה הִיא. וַאֲפִלּוּ בָאוּ עָלֶיהָ עֲשָׂרָה בְנֵי אָדָם כֻּלָּם שֶׁלֹּא כְדַרְכָּהּ, כֻּלָּם בִּסְקִילָה:


בתולה – and not a woman who is no longer a virgin, and if she had intercourse in an unnatural manner, she is still a virgin, and even if ten men came upon her altogether in an unnatural manner, they would all be punished by stoning.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:9:3)


**מְאֹרָסָה.** וְלֹא נְשׂוּאָה:


מאורסה – but not married.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:9:4)


**וְהִיא בְבֵית אָבִיהָ.** שֶׁאִם מָסַר הָאָב לִשְׁלוּחֵי הַבַּעַל, הַבָּא עָלֶיהָ אַחַר כָּךְ אֵינוֹ בִסְקִילָה אֶלָּא בְחֶנֶק:


והיא בבית אביה – but if the father sent [her] to the agents of the husband, and he came upon her afterwards, [his punishment] is not by stoning, but by choking.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:10:1)


**זֶה הֶדְיוֹט שֶׁהֵסִית.** דַּוְקָא הֶדְיוֹט שֶׁהֵסִית. שֶׁנָּבִיא הַמֵּסִית מִיתָתוֹ בְחֶנֶק:


זה ההדיוט המסית – specifically an ordinary person who beguiles, as a prophet who beguiles, his death is by choking.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:10:2)


**וְהַמֵּסִית אֶת הַהֶדְיוֹט.** לָאו דַּוְקָא, שֶׁלֹּא מָצִינוּ חִלּוּק בֵּין מֵסִית אֶת הַהֶדְיוֹט לְמֵסִית אֶת הַנָּבִיא. אֶלָּא לִמְעוּטֵי מֵסִית אֶת הָרַבִּים, כְּגוֹן מַדִּיחֵי עִיר מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁמִּיתָתָן בְּחֶנֶק:


והמסית את ההדיוט – not exactly, for we found no distinction between a person who entices an ordinary person to those who entices a prophet, but to exclude those who entice the many, such as those who lead astray a city in Israel, that their deaths are by choking.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:10:3)


**מַכְמִינִין.** לְשׁוֹן מַאֲרָב:


מכמינין – the language of lying in wait.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:10:4)


**אִם הָיָה הַמֵּסִית עָרוּם וְאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְדַבֵּר בִּפְנֵיהֶם.** כְּלוֹמַר אוֹמֵר לַנִּסָּת שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְדַבֵּר בִּפְנֵיהֶם מִפְּנֵי יִרְאַת בֵּית דִּין:


אם היה המסית ערום ואינו יכול לדבר בפניהם – that is to say, he says to the enticed that he cannot speak in their presence because of fear of the Jewish court.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:10:5)


**בְּיִחוּד.** כְּלוֹמַר, אֵין אִישׁ עִמָּנוּ וְיָכוֹל אַתָּה לוֹמַר עַתָּה מַה שֶּׁאָמַרְתָּ לִי כְבָר:


ביחוד – that is to say, there is no person with us and you can say now what you have told me already.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:10:6)


**הָאוֹמֵר אֶעֱבֹד.** בְּאֶחָד מִכָּל הַלְּשׁוֹנוֹת הַלָּלוּ הָוֵי מֵסִית וְחַיָּב:


האומר אעבוד – in one of these linguistic formulations, he is an enticer and is liable.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:10:7)


**הַמַּדִּיחַ.** וְאִם מַדִּיחַ אֶת הָרַבִּים הוּא, אֵינוֹ חַיָּב עַד שֶׁיֹּאמַר בִּלְשׁוֹן רַבִּים נֵלֵךְ וְנַעֲבֹד:


המדיח – if he led the many astray, he is not guilty until he says in the plural form: “let us go and worship.”



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:11:1)


**הָעוֹשֶׂה מַעֲשֶׂה.** מַמָּשׁ, בִּסְקִילָה:


העושה מעשה – an actual [deed], [is punished] by stoning.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:11:2)


**וְלֹא הָאוֹחֵז אֶת הָעֵינָיִם.** מַרְאֶה לַבְּרִיּוֹת כְּאִלּוּ עוֹשֶׂה, וְאֵינוֹ עוֹשֶׂה כְלוּם:


ולא האוחז את העינים – shows to other creatures as if he is doing, but is not actually doing anything.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 7:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/7:11:3)


**שְׁנַיִם לוֹקְטִים קִשּׁוּאִים.** בִּמְכַשְּׁפוּת בְּפָנֵינוּ, אֶחָד מֵהֶן לָקַט וְחַיָּב מִיתָה, וַחֲבֵרוֹ לָקַט וּפָטוּר מִן הַמִּיתָה. כֵּיצַד, הָעוֹשֶׂה מַעֲשֶׂה שֶׁלָּקַט מַמָּשׁ בִּכְשָׁפִים, חַיָּב. וְהָאוֹחֵז אֶת הָעֵינַיִם, הֶרְאָה לָנוּ כְּאִלּוּ נִתְקַבְּצוּ כֻלָּן לְמָקוֹם אֶחָד וְהַקִּשּׁוּאִים לֹא זָזוּ מִמְּקוֹמָן, פָּטוּר מִן הַמִּיתָה:


שנים לוקטים קשואים – through witchcraft in our presence. One of them harvested and is liable for the death penalty, and his fellow harvested and is exempt from death. How so? He wo performed the deed of harvesting actually through witchcraft, is liable. האוחז את העינים – he showed us as if they were all gathered together in one place and the cucumbers did not move from their places. פטור – [exempt] from the death penalty.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:1:1)


**בֵּן סוֹרֵר וּמוֹרֶה.** מִשֶּׁיָּבִיא שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת. וְהוּא בֶן שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה וְיוֹם אֶחָד. דְּמִקַמֵּי הָכִי אֵין הַשְּׂעָרוֹת סִימָן אֶלָּא שׁוּמָא:


בן סורר ומורה. משיביא שתי שערות – when he brings forth two [pubic] hairs, and he is thirteen years of age and one day old, for before this, the hairs are not a sign, but rather an estimation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:1:2)


**עַד שֶׁיַּקִּיף זָקָן הַתַּחְתּוֹן.** הַאי זָקָן דְּאַמוּר רַבָּנָן, הַתַּחְתּוֹן אָמְרוּ, שֶׁיַּקִּיף הַשֵּׂעָר סְבִיבוֹת הַגִּיד:


עד שיקיף זקן התחתון – this “beard” that the Rabbis speak about, the lower one they spoke about that the hair surrounds the membrum virile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:1:3)


**וְלֹא הָעֶלְיוֹן.** זָקָן מַמָּשׁ:


ולא העליון – the beard itself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:1:4)


**שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בֵּן וְלֹא אִישׁ.** מִשֶּׁהִקִּיף זָקָן הַתַּחְתּוֹן אִישׁ הוּא. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּמִקַּטְנוּתוֹ הוּא קָרוּי בֵּן, לֹא מָצִינוּ לְחַיְּבוֹ מִקַמֵּי שֶׁיָּבִיא שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת, דְּקָטָן פָּטוּר, שֶׁלֹּא בָא לִכְלַל הַמִּצְוֹת, הִלְכָּךְ חִיּוּבֵיהּ בָּתַר הָכִי הוּא. וְהָכִי קָאָמַר קְרָא, כִּי יִהְיֶה לְאִישׁ בֵּן, בֵּן הַסָּמוּךְ לִגְבוּרָתוֹ שֶׁל אִישׁ, בִּתְחִלַּת אִישׁוּתוֹ חַיְּבֵיהּ קְרָא. וּמִשֶּׁהִקִּיף זָקָן הַתַּחְתּוֹן, אִישׁ גָּמוּר הוּא:


שנאמר בן ולא איש – where the lower “beard” surrounds it, it is a man, and even though from his youth he is called a son, we do not find him to be liable before he brings for two [pubic] hairs for a minor is exempt [from being a stubborn and rebellious son], for he has not come in to the general [category] of the commandments; therefore, they obligate him after this. And this is what the Bible (Deuteronomy 21:18) states: “When a man has a [wayward and stubborn] son…,” a son who is close to his might as an adult male, at the beginning of his manhood, the Bible makes liable, and when his lower “beard” surrounds [it], he is a complete [adult] man.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:1)


**טַרְטֵימַר.** חֲצִי מָנֶה. וְהוּא שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַבָּשָׂר בָּשִׁיל וְלֹא בָשִׁיל, כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁהַלִּסְטִים אוֹכְלִים:


טרטימר – one-half a Maneh (according to Sanhedrin 70a), and that is meat that is cooked and not cooked (-semi-cooked), in the manner that the robbers eat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:2)


**מִן הַיַּיִן הָאִיטַלְקִי.** שֶׁהוּא מְשֻׁבָּח וּמִמְּשִׁיךְ בָּתְרֵיהּ. וְהוּא דְשָׁתֵי לֵיהּ מְזִיג וְלֹא מְזִיג:


מן היין האיטלקי – which is praiseworthy and prolongs [the meal] after it, and it is one that a person drinks mixed [with water] and not mixed [with water] (-semi-moxed).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:3)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


רבי יוסי אומר כו' – But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yosi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:4)


**אָכַל בַּחֲבוּרַת מִצְוָה.** בִּסְעֻדָּה שֶׁל מִצְוָה:


אבל בחבורת מצוה – in a meal [celebrating the performance] of a Mitzvah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:5)


**אָכַל בְּעִבּוּר הַחֹדֶשׁ.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּאֵין עוֹלִין לְאוֹתָהּ סְעֻדָּה אֶלָּא בְּפַת וְקִטְנִית בִּלְבָד וְאִיהוּ אַסִּיק בָּשָׂר וְיַיִן, הוֹאִיל וּבְמִצְוָה קָעָסֵיק לֹא מִמְּשִׁיךְ:


אבל עיבור החדש – even though we do not serve for that meal other than bread and pulse/beans/peas, and he offers meat and wine, since he is involved in performing a Mitzvah, he doesn’t continue.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:6)


**אָכַל מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם.** כֵּיוָן דִּכְדֶרֶךְ מִצְוָתוֹ הוּא, דִּכְתִיב בְּמַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי (דברים יד) בַּבָּקָר וּבַצֹּאן וּבַיַּיִן וּבַשֵּׁכָר, לֹא מִמְּשִׁיךְ:


אבל מעשר שני בירושלים – since it is the manner of the Mitzvah, as it is written regarding the Second Tithe (Deuteronomy 14:26): “[And spend the money on anything you want] – cattle, sheep, wine or other intoxicant – [or anything you may desire….],” and he doesn’t continue.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:7)


**נְבֵלוֹת וּטְרֵפוֹת שְׁקָצִים וּרְמָשִׂים.** דִּכְתִיב אֵינֶנּוּ שֹׁמֵעַ בְּקֹלֵנוּ, וְלֹא זֶה שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ בְקוֹלוֹ שֶׁל הַמָּקוֹם בָּרוּךְ הוּא אֵינוֹ שׁוֹמֵעַ:


נבילות וטריפות שקצים ורמשים – as it is written (Deuteronomy 21:20): “[This son of ours is disloyal and defiant;] he does not heed us…,” and it is not this, for even does not listen to the voice of God.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:8)


**דָּבָר שֶׁהוּא מִצְוָה.** דְּרַבָּנָן, לַאֲתוֹיֵי תַּנְחוּמֵי אֲבֵלִים. דְּאִי מֵרֵישָׁא, הֲוָה אֲמֵינָא חֲבוּרַת מִצְוָה הַיְנוּ כֹהֲנִים שֶׁאוֹכְלִים קָדָשִׁים אוֹ אֲכִילַת פְּסָחִים:


דבר שהוא מצוה – of the Rabbis, which includes comforting the bereaved, for if it were from the beginning of the Mishnah, I would think that an association formed for a religious duty, that is, Kohanim who eat their sacred portions or the consumption of the Passover offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:2:9)


**וְדָבָר שֶׁהוּא עֲבֵרָה.** לַאֲתוֹיֵי תַּעֲנִית צִבּוּר שֶׁאִסּוּרוֹ מִדִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים:


ודבר שהוא עבירה – to include a community fast, whose prohibition is from the words of the Scribes.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:3:1)


**גָּנַב מִשֶּׁל אָבִיו וְאָכַל בִּרְשׁוּת אָבִיו.** אַף עַל גַּב דִּשְׁכִיחַ לֵיהּ לִגְנֹב מִשֶּׁל אָבִיו, הוֹאִיל וְאָכַל בִּרְשׁוּת אָבִיו מִתְפַּחֵד וּמִתְבַּעֵת מֵאָבִיו שֶׁמָּא יִרְאֶנּוּ, וְלֹא מִמְּשִׁיךְ:


גנב משל אביו ואכל ברשות אביו – even though it is frequent for him to steal from his father, since and he ate in his father’s domain, he is afraid of and agitated by his father, lest he see him and not continue.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:3:2)


**גָּנַב מִשֶּׁל אֲחֵרִים.** לֹא שְׁכִיחַ לֵיהּ שֶׁיּוּכַל בְּכָל עֵת לִגְנֹב מִשֶּׁל אֲחֵרִים, וְלֹא מִמְּשִׁיךְ:


גנב משל אחרים – it is not frequent for him that he would be able at any time to steal from others, and he doesn’t continue.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:3:3)


**עַד שֶׁיִּגְנֹב מִשֶּׁל אָבִיו.** דִּשְׁכִיחַ לֵיהּ לִגְנֹב בְּכָל עֵת:


עד שיגנוב משל אביו – since it is frequent for him to steal at all times.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:3:4)


**וְיֹאכַל בִּרְשׁוּת אֲחֵרִים.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִתְבַּעֵת שָׁם מֵאָבִיו, דְּבִכְהַאי גַּוְנָא וַדַּאי מִמְּשִׁיךְ:


ויאכל ברשות אחרים – for he is not agitated there from his father, in a such a manner, and with a certainty, he continues.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:3:5)


**וּמִשֶּׁל אִמּוֹ.** מִנְּכָסִים שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ שֶׁאֵין לְאָבִיו רְשׁוּת בָּהֶן. כְּגוֹן שֶׁנָּתַן לָהּ אַחֵר נְכָסִים בְּמַתָּנָה עַל מְנָת שֶׁאֵין לְבַעְלָהּ רְשׁוּת בָּהֶן. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי בְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ומשל אמו – from the possessions that she has that his father has no control over them, such as if another person gave her possessions as a gift on the condition that her husband has no control over them. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yosi B’Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:4:1)


**רְאוּיָה לְאָבִיו.** שָׁוָה וְדוֹמָה לְאָבִיו בְּקוֹל בְּמַרְאֶה וְקוֹמָה. דִּכְתִיב אֵינֶנּוּ שֹׁמֵעַ בְּקֹלֵנוּ, מִדְּלֹא כְתִיב בְּקוֹלוֹתֵינוּ, שְׁמַע מִנַּהּ קוֹל אֶחָד לִשְׁנֵיהֶם. וּמִדְקוֹל בָּעִינַן שָׁוִים, מַרְאֶה וְקוֹמָה נַמִּי בָּעִינַן שָׁוִים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ראויה לאביו – equivalent and similar to his father in voice, in appearance and in stature, as it is written (Deuteronomy 21:20): “…he does not heed us,” since it is not written [in the Torah]: “in our voices.” We learn from this, one voice for both. And from the voice, we require that it be equivalent. Appearance and stature also we require equivalence. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:4:2)


**גִּדֵּם.** יָדוֹ קְטוּעָה:


גידם – his hand is lopped off.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:4:3)


**בְּנֵנוּ זֶה.** מַשְׁמַע שֶׁמַּרְאִין אוֹתוֹ:


בננו זה – implying that we cause to see him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:4:4)


**חֵרְשִׁים.** אִם אָמַר לָהֶם אֵינִי מְקַבֵּל מִכֶּם אֵינָם שׁוֹמְעִים אוֹתוֹ. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁרוֹאִים אַחַר כָּךְ שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְקַיֵּם מִצְוָתָן, מִכָּל מָקוֹם הַתּוֹרָה אָמְרָה אֵינֶנּוּ שׁוֹמֵעַ בְּקוֹלֵנוּ, דְּמַשְׁמַע דְּבִשְׁעַת הַקּוֹל קָאָמְרִי דְּלֹא שָׁמַע, שֶׁשְּׁמָעוּהוּ שֶׁאָמַר אֵינִי מְקַבֵּל מִכֶּם:


חרשים – if he said to them: “I don’t accept [this] from you and they do not hear him. And even though they see afterwards, that he does not fulfill their commands. Nevertheless, the Torah stated (Deuteronomy 21:20): “...he does not heed us,” which implies at the time of voice when they speak, that he doesn’t listen, since they heard him say, “I do not accept [this] from you.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:4:5)


**וּמַתְרִין בּוֹ בִּפְנֵי שְׁלֹשָׁה.** הָכִי קָאָמַר, וּמַתְרִין בּוֹ בִּפְנֵי שְׁנַיִם שֶׁלֹּא יַרְגִּיל. וְאִם לֹא שָׁמַע, מַלְקִין אוֹתוֹ בְבֵית דִּין שֶׁל שְׁלֹשָׁה, כְּדִתְנַן בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא, מַכּוֹת בִּשְׁלשָׁה. דִּוְיִסְּרוּ אוֹתוֹ הָאָמוּר בְּבֵן סוֹרֵר וּמוֹרֶה מַלְקוֹת הוּא, כְּתִיב הָכָא (דברים כא) בֵּן סוֹרֵר וּמוֹרֶה, וּכְתִיב הָתָם (שם כה) וְהָיָה אִם בִּן הַכּוֹת הָרָשָׁע:


ומתרין בו בפני שלשה– This is how this should be understood: And they warn him before two [witnesses] that he should not become accustomed, and if he did not listen, they would flog him with a Jewish court of three, as is taught [in the Mishnah] of the first chapter [of Sanhedrin] (Mishnah 2): “Flogging – through a court of three. That (Deuteronomy 21:18): “even after they discipline him,” which is stated in the case of the wayward and defiant son [refers to] flogging. It is written here (Deuteronomy 21:18): “A wayward and defiant son” and it is written there (Deuteronomy 25:2): “If the guilty one is to be flogged…”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:4:6)


**זֶה הוּא שֶׁלָּקָה בִּפְנֵיכֶם.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּמִבָּעֵי לֵיהּ זֶה וְלֹא סוּמִין, אִי אִיתָא דִלְהַךְ מִלְּתָא לְחוֹד אֲתָא, לְזֶה הוּא שֶׁלָּקָה בִּפְנֵיכֶם, לִכְתּוּב בְּנֵנוּ הוּא, מַאי בְּנֵנוּ זֶה, שָׁמַעְתְּ מִנַּהּ תַּרְתֵּי:


זה הוא שלקה בפניכם – and even though this is required for excluding blind person, if there is for this thing alone that he comes, for this one that he is flogged in their presence, [the Bible] should have written, “he is our son.” What is “this son of ours” (Deuteronomy 21:20)? You learn two things from it:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:4:7)


**וְאַחַר כָּךְ הִקִּיף זָקָן הַתַּחְתּוֹן פָּטוּר.** כֵּיוָן דְּאִלּוּ עֲבִיד הַשְׁתָּא לָאו בַּר קְטָלָא הוּא:


ואח"כ הקיף זקן התחתון פטור – since that he had ac ted now, he is not guilty of a capital crime.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:4:8)


**וְאִם מִשֶּׁנִּגְמַר דִּינוֹ בָּרַח.** הָוֵי כְגַבְרָא קְטִילָא וְחַיָּב אֲפִלּוּ לְאַחַר כַּמָּה שָׁנִים:


ואם משנגמר דינו ברכח – he is like a man who is to be put to death, and is guilty, even after a few years.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:1)


**נִדּוֹן עַל שֵׁם סוֹפוֹ.** סוֹף שֶׁמְּכַלֶּה מָמוֹן אָבִיו וּמְבַקֵּשׁ מַה שֶּׁהֻרְגַּל בּוֹ וְאֵינוֹ מוֹצֵא, וְיוֹשֵׁב בְּפָרָשַׁת דְּרָכִים וּמְלַסְטֵם אֶת הַבְּרִיּוֹת, אָמְרָה תוֹרָה יָמוּת זַכַּאי וְאַל יָמוּת חַיָּב:


נדון על שם סופו – at the end when he uses up the money of his father and requests what he is accustomed to and he cannot find and sits at the intersections and robs people. The Torah stated that he should die innocent and should not die guilty.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:2)


**הֲנָאָה לָהֶן.** שֶׁאֵינָן מוֹסִיפִין לַחֲטֹא:


הנאה להן – that they do not add to sin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:3)


**וַהֲנָאָה לָעוֹלָם.** שָׁקְטָה כָל הָאָרֶץ:


והנאה לעולם – the whole world became quiet.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:4)


**רַע לָהֶן.** שֶׁהָיוּ מוֹסִיפִים זְכֻיּוֹת:


רע להן – that they would add merits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:5)


**וְרַע לָעוֹלָם.** שֶׁהָיוּ מְגִנִּים עַל דּוֹרָן וּמוֹכִיחִין אֶת הַדּוֹר:


ורע לעולם – that they would defend their generation and reprove the generation


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:6)


**יַיִן וְשֵׁנָה לָרְשָׁעִים.** כָּל זְמַן שֶׁשּׁוֹתִים וִישֵׁנִים אֵין חוֹטְאִין וְאֵין מְרֵעִין לַבְּרִיּוֹת:


יין ושינה לרשעים – all that time that they drink and sleep, they don’t sin and they don’t do evil things to other people.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:7)


**לַצַּדִּיקִים רַע לָהֶן.** שֶׁאֵינָם עוֹסְקִין בַּתּוֹרָה:


לצדיקים רע להן – that they do not engage in Torah [study].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:8)


**וְרַע לָעוֹלָם.** שֶׁכְּשֶׁהֵן מִתְבַּטְּלִים, פֻּרְעָנוּת בָּאָה לָעוֹלָם:


ורע לעולם – when they are idle, retribution comes to the world.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:5:9)


**פִּזּוּר.** שֶׁהֵן נִפְרָדִים זֶה מִזֶּה וְאֵין יְכוֹלִים לְהִוָּעֵץ וּלְסַיֵּעַ זֶה אֶת זֶה:


פיזור – when they are separated one from the other and they are unable to advise and help one another.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:6:1)


**הַבָּא בַמַּחְתֶּרֶת.** שֶׁאָמְרָה תוֹרָה יֵהָרֵג:


הבא במחתרת – The Torah stated (Exodus 22:1, Sanhedrin 72a) that he should be killed (i.e., beaten to death).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:6:2)


**נִדּוֹן עַל שֵׁם סוֹפוֹ.** שֶׁסּוֹפוֹ לַהֲרֹג אֶת בַּעַל הַבַּיִת אִם יַעֲמֹד כְּנֶגְדּוֹ לְהַצִּיל אֶת שֶׁלּוֹ:


נדון על שם סופו – that his end is to kill the house owner if he should against him to save what is his.,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:6:3)


**אִם יֶשׁ לוֹ דָמִים חַיָּב.** כְּגוֹן הָאָב הַבָּא בַמַּחְתֶּרֶת עַל הַבֵּן, בְּיָדוּעַ שֶׁרַחֲמֵי הָאָב עַל הַבֵּן, לְפִיכָךְ אֵין הַבֵּן רַשַּׁאי לְהָרְגוֹ. וְאִם שָׁבַר הָאָב הֶחָבִית, חַיָּב לְשַׁלֵּם:


אם יש לו דמים חייב – such as when the father comes in the breach over his son, and it is known that the father has compassion on his son. Therefore, the son is not permitted to kill him, and if the father broke the barrel, he is obligated to pay.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:6:4)


**אֵין לוֹ דָמִים פָּטוּר.** וּשְׁאָר כָּל אָדָם הַבָּא בַמַּחְתֶּרֶת שֶׁאִם הֲרָגוֹ בַעַל הַבַּיִת אֵין לוֹ דָמִים, אִם שָׁבַר אֶת הֶחָבִית פָּטוּר מִלְּשַׁלֵּם. שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁהוּא מִתְחַיֵּב בְּנַפְשׁוֹ פָּטוּר מִן הַתַּשְׁלוּמִין, שֶׁאֵין אָדָם מֵת וּמְשַׁלֵּם:


אין לו דמים פטור – and all other peole who go into the breach, if the house owner did not kill him, he has no bloodguilt.. And if he broker the barrel, he is exempt from payment, since he was liable for his life, he is exempt from payment, for a person does not both die and make payment. (Note: the punishment that is made is the more severe: (קם ליה מדרבה מיניה).



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:7:1)


**וְאֵלּוּ הֵן שֶׁמַּצִּילִין אוֹתָן בְּנַפְשָׁן.** שֶׁמַּצִּילִין אוֹתָן מִן הָעֲבֵרָה בְּנַפְשָׁן, שֶׁנִּתַּן רְשׁוּת לְכָל אָדָם לְהָרְגָם כְּדֵי לְהַצִּילָן מִן הָעֲבֵרָה:


ואלו שמצילין אותן בנפשן – that we save them from [committing] sin. בנפשן – for permission is granted to every person to kill them to save them from [committing] sin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:7:2)


**הָרוֹדֵף אַחַר חֲבֵרוֹ לְהָרְגוֹ.** דִּכְתִיב אֵצֶל נַעֲרָה הַמְאֹרָסָה (דברים כב) כִּי כַּאֲשֶׁר יָקוּם אִישׁ עַל רֵעֵהוּ וּרְצָחוֹ נֶפֶשׁ כֵּן הַדָּבָר הַזֶּה, מַקִּישׁ רוֹצֵחַ לְנַעֲרָה מְאֹרָסָה, מַה נַּעֲרָה מְאֹרָסָה נִתָּן לְהַצִּילָהּ בְּנַפְשׁוֹ, אַף רוֹצֵחַ נִתָּן לְהַצִּילוֹ בְנַפְשׁוֹ. וְנַעֲרָה מְאֹרָסָה נָפְקָא לָן מִקְּרָא דִּכְתִיב (שם) צָעֲקָה הַנַּעֲרָה הַמְאֹרָשָׂה וְאֵין מוֹשִׁיעַ לָהּ, הָא יֵשׁ מוֹשִׁיעַ לָהּ חַיָּב לְהוֹשִׁיעָהּ בְּכָל דָּבָר שֶׁיָּכוֹל לְהַצִּילָהּ:


הרודף אחר חבירו להרגו – as it is written regarding the engaged maiden (Deuteronomy 22:26): “[But you shall do nothing to the girl. The girl did not incur the death penalty,] for this case is like that of a man attacking another and murdering him.” An analogy is made [between] the killer and the betrothed maiden. Just as the betrothed maiden is given/intended to have her purity saved by killing the rapists, so too in the case of a killer the persecuted person is given to be saved by killing the killers. And the betrothed maiden is derived from Scripture as it is written (Deuteronomy 22:27): “[He came upon her in the open;] though the engaged girl cried for help, there was no one to save her,” behold there is someone to save her, one is obligated to save her in any manner that one can save her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:7:3)


**וְאַחַר הַזְּכוּר.** נָפְקָא לָן דְּאָמַר קְרָא (שם) וְלַנַּעֲרָה לֹא תַעֲשֶׂה דָבָר, נַעַר כְּתִיב חָסֵר הֵ"א, זֶה זְכוּר. וְהוּא הַדִּין לְכָל חַיָּבֵי כְרֵתוֹת וּמִיתוֹת בֵּית דִּין שֶׁבַּעֲרָיוֹת, שֶׁמַּצִּילִין אוֹתָן בְּנַפְשָׁן, דִּכְתִיב (שם) חֵטְא מָוֶת. חֵטְא, אֵלּוּ חַיָּבֵי כְרֵתוֹת. מָוֶת, אֵלּוּ חַיָּבֵי מִיתוֹת בֵּית דִּין:


ואחד הזכור – we derive it as it says in Scripture (Deuteronomy 22:26): “But you shall do nothing to the girl.” The word "נער" /”girl” is written missing a [letter] "ה"/”hei.” This is the male, and the same law applies for all though liable for extirpation and death by the Jewish court for forbidden sexual relations that we save them, as it is written (Deuteronomy 22:26): “[The girl did not] incur the death penalty…” [The word] "חטא"/”sin”/penalty – these are the ones liable for extirpation; [the word] "מות"/”death,” – these are ones liable to death by the Jewish court.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:7:4)


**אֲבָל הָרוֹדֵף אַחַר הַבְּהֵמָה.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁדּוֹמֶה לַעֲרָיוֹת:


אבל הרודף אחר הבהמה – even though it is similar to incest/consanguinity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:7:5)


**וְהַמְחַלֵּל אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת וְהָעוֹבֵד עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁשְּׁנֵיהֶם כּוֹפְרִים בָּעִקָּר:


והמחלל שבת והעובד עבודה זרה – even though that both of them deny the essence [of God].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 8:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/8:7:6)


**אֵין מַצִּילִין אוֹתָם בְּנַפְשָׁן.** וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן שְׁאָר חַיָּבֵי כְרֵתוֹת וּמִיתוֹת בֵּית דִּין שֶׁאֵינָן שֶׁל עֲרָיוֹת, שֶׁאֵין מַצִּילִין אוֹתָם בְּנַפְשָׁם, וְאֵין מֻתָּר לְהָרְגָן כְּלָל עַד שֶׁיַּעַבְרוּ עֲבֵרָה בְעֵדִים וְיִהְיוּ חַיָּבִים מִיתָה בְּבֵית דִּין:


אין מצילין אותם בנפשן – And all the more so, the rest of those who are liable to extirpation and death at the hands of the Jewish court which are not incest/consanguinity, which we do not save them (from sin) by killing them, and it is not permitted to kill them at all until they transgress a sin with witnesses [testifying] and would be liable to death by the Jewish court.



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:1)


**וְאֵלּוּ הֵן הַנִּשְׂרָפִים.** הַבָּא עַל אִשָּׁה וּבִתָּהּ. עַל אִשָּׁה שֶׁכְּבָר נָשָׂא בִתָּהּ, דְּהַיְנוּ חֲמוֹתוֹ:


אלו הן הנשרפין. הבא על אשה ובתה – on a woman whose daughter has already married, that is his mother-in-law.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:2)


**וּבַת כֹּהֵן.** וְכֵן בַּת כֹּהֵן שֶׁזִּנְּתָה הִיא בִשְׂרֵפָה:


ובת כהן – and the daughter of a Kohen who ran about as a prostitute/was unchaste, [whose punishment] is by swallowing molten liquid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:3)


**יֵשׁ בִּכְלָל אִשָּׁה וּבִתָּהּ.** כְּלוֹמַר בְּאִשָּׁה וּבִתָּהּ כְּתִיבָא שְׂרֵפָה בְּהֶדְיָא (ויקרא כ) וְאִישׁ אֲשֶׁר יִקַּח אֶת אִשָּׁה וְאֶת אִמָּהּ וְגוֹ'‏ בָּאֵשׁ יִשְׂרְפוּ, וּמִנַּהּ יָלְפִינַן כֻּלְּהוּ הָנָךְ נַמִּי דְּאִיתְנְהוּ בִשְׂרֵפָה:


יש בכלל אשה ובתה – that is to say, with a mother and her daughter, it is explicitly written "שריפה"/burning (Leviticus 20:14): “If a man marries a woman and her mother….they shall be put to the fire/"באש תשרפו" …,” and from it we derive that all of these also and it is they are [punished] by burning.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:4)


**בִּתּוֹ.** מֵאֲנוּסָתוֹ, דְּלָאו בַּת אִשְׁתּוֹ הִיא דִּלְחַיַּב עָלֶיהָ מִשּׁוּם בַּת אִשְׁתּוֹ, דְּהָא לָאו בַּת אִשְׁתּוֹ הִיא:


בתו – from an outraged woman, for she is not the daughter of his wife, and he is liable on her because of his wife’s daughter, but she is not his wife’s daughter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:5)


**בַּת בִּתּוֹ וּבַת בְּנוֹ.** שֶׁהָיוּ לוֹ מֵאֲנוּסָתוֹ:


בת בתו ובת בנו – for they were his from his being a victim of unavoidably prevented accident.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:6)


**בַּת אִשְׁתּוֹ.** בֵּין שֶׁהִיא בִתּוֹ בֵּין שֶׁהִיא חוֹרַגְתּוֹ:


בת אשתו – whether she is his daughter or whether she is his step-daughter.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:7)


**וַחֲמוֹתוֹ.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּתָנָא לַהּ רֵישָׁא בְהֶדְיָא הַבָּא עַל אִשָּׁה שֶׁנָּשָׂא בִתָּהּ, וְלֹא מִדְּרָשָׁא אָתְיָא, אַיְדֵי דְתָנֵי בְהַךְ כְּלָלָא אֵם חֲמוֹתוֹ וְאֵם חָמִיו דְּאָתְיָא מִדְּרָשָׁא, תָּנָא נַמִּי אַגַּב רִהֲטָא חֲמוֹתוֹ בַּהֲדַיְהוּ:


וחמותו – even though that it [the Mishnah] explicitly taught at the first section – he who has relations with a woman who has married her daughter, and it is does not come from a derivation, for since it was taught regarding these a general principle – for the mother of his mother-in-law and the mother of his father-in-law, that come from a derivation, the Mishnah also taught, by the way, regarding his mother-in-law with them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:8)


**כָּבַשׁ עָלָיו.** אָחַז רֹאשׁוֹ שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ וּתְקָפוֹ בְתוֹךְ הַמַּיִם שֶׁלֹּא יוּכַל לְהָרִים רֹאשׁוֹ:


כבש עליו – he grabbed the head of his fellow and held him firmly in the water so that he wasn’t able to raise his head.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:9)


**שִׁסָּה.** גֵּרָה:


שיסה – incite/let loose


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:10)


**הִשִּׁיךְ.** שֶׁאָחַז הַנָּחָשׁ בְּיָדוֹ וְהוֹלִיכוֹ וְהִגִּיעַ שִׁנֵּי הַנָּחָשׁ לַגּוּף שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ:


השיך – that he held the snake in his hand and led him and the tenth of the snake reached the body of his fellow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:11)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה מְחַיֵּב.** דְּסָבַר אֶרֶס נָחָשׁ בְּשִׁנָּיו עוֹמֵד, וּמִכֵּיוָן שֶׁהִגִּיעַ שִׁנֵּי הַנָּחָשׁ בִּבְשָׂרוֹ שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ הָוֵי כְאִלּוּ הֲרָגוֹ וְחַיָּב:


רבי יהודה מחייב – that he holds that the poison of the snake in its teeth stands, and since that the tenth of the snake arrived in the skin of his fellow, it is as if he had killed him, and is liable.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:12)


**וַחֲכָמִים פּוֹטְרִין.** דְּקָא סָבְרֵי אֶרֶס נָחָשׁ מֵעַצְמוֹ הוּא מֵקִיא, הִלְכָּךְ לָאו אִיהוּ קַטְלֵיהּ אֶלָּא גְרָמָא בְעָלְמָא הוּא וּפָטוּר. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים פוטרין – for they hold that the snake himself vomits his poison; therefore, it was not he that killed him but rather a mere indirect effect and he is exempt. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:13)


**וַאֲמָדוּהוּ לְמִיתָה.** אֲבָל אֲמָדוּהוּ תְחִלָּה לְחַיִּים, אֲפִלּוּ לְרַבָּנָן פָּטוּר:


ואמדוהו למיתה – but we appraise him first for life. Even to the Rabbis, he is exempt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:14)


**וְהֵקֵל מִמַּה שֶּׁהָיָה.** וְחָזְרוּ וַאֲמָדוּהוּ לְחַיִּים:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:1:15)


**שֶׁרַגְלַיִם לַדָּבָר.** שֶׁלֹּא מֵת מֵחֲמַת מַכָּה זוֹ:


שרגלים לדבר – that he didn’t die on account of that wound.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:2:1)


**וְהָיָה בָהּ כְּדֵי לְהָמִית עַל לִבּוֹ וָמֵת פָּטוּר.** דְּתַרְתֵּי בָעִינַן, שֶׁיְּהֵא מִתְכַּוֵּן לְמַכַּת מִיתָה וְגַם שֶׁיַּכֶּנּוּ מַכַּת מִיתָה:


והיה בה כדי להמית על לבו ומת פטור – That both are necessary, that he intends to wound [to cause] death and also that they should stripe him a wound [to cause] death.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:2:2)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר אֲפִלּוּ נִתְכַּוֵּן לַהֲרֹג אֶת זֶה.** לָאו אַסֵּיפָא דְמִלְּתָא דְתַנָּא קַמָּא קָאֵי, אַהָא דְקָאָמַר תַּנָּא קַמָּא נִתְכַּוֵּן לְהַכּוֹת אֶת הַגָּדוֹל וְהָיָה בָהּ כְּדֵי לְהָמִית אֶת הַגָּדוֹל וְהָלְכָה עַל הַקָּטָן וָמֵת חַיָּב, דְּאִי אַהָא קָאֵי, רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן פּוֹטֵר מִבָּעֵי לֵיהּ, וְלָמָּה לֵּיהּ לְמִהְדַּר וּלְפָרוֹשֵׁי אֲפִלּוּ נִתְכַּוֵּן לַהֲרֹג אֶת זֶה וְהָרַג אֶת זֶה, הָא בְּפֵרוּשׁ אֲמָרוֹ תַנָּא קַמָּא, וּמַאי אֲפִלּוּ. אֶלָּא רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אַרֵישָׁא קָאֵי, נִתְכַּוֵּן לַהֲרֹג אֶת הַבְּהֵמָה וְהָרַג אֶת הָאָדָם פָּטוּר, הָא נִתְכַּוֵּן לַהֲרֹג אֶת הָאָדָם וְהָרַג אָדָם אַחֵר חַיָּב, וַעֲלַהּ קָאָמַר רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אֲפִלּוּ נִתְכַּוֵּן לַהֲרֹג אֶת זֶה וְהָרַג אֶת זֶה פָּטוּר. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ר"ש אומר אפילו נתכוין להרוג את זה. – this does not refer to the end of the matter of the first Tanna, on that which the first Tanna [mentioned]– that he had the intention to wound the large individual but if it struck the small individual and he died, he is liable. For it is upon this that it refers, Rabbi Shimon exempts this person, is needed to be said, and why should he retract and explain that even if he intended to kill this one and killed that one, as the first Tanna explicitly stated this, what is the purpose of the word “even?” But, Rabbi Shimon is referring to the beginning – that if he intended to kill the animal and [instead] killed a person, he is exempt; but if he intended to kill a [particular] person but [instead] killed another person, he is liable. And on this, Rabbi Shimon comments – even if he intended to kill this one and killed [instead] that one, he is exempt. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:3:1)


**רוֹצֵחַ שֶׁנִּתְעָרֵב בַּאֲחֵרִים.** כְּגוֹן שְׁנַיִם שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹמְדִים וְיָצָא חֵץ מִבֵּינֵיהֶם וְהָרַג, שְׁנֵיהֶם פְּטוּרִים. וַאֲפִלּוּ אֶחָד מִשְּׁנֵיהֶם מֻחְזָק בַּחֲסִידוּת וּבְיָדוּעַ שֶׁהוּא לֹא זָרַק הַחֵץ, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי לֹא מְחַיְּבִין לֵיהּ לְאִידָךְ בַּחֲזָקָה זוֹ:


רוצח שנתערב באחרים – such as two people who were standing and an arrow came out from between them and killed, both of them are exempt, and even if one of the two of them was presumed for his righteousness and it was known that he did not shoot the arrow, even so, they don’t make him liabler for other by this presumption.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:3:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר כּוֹנְסִין אוֹתָן לְכִפָּה.** מַתְנִיתִין חֲסוּרֵי מִחַסְּרָא וְהָכִי קָתָנֵי, וְשׁוֹר שֶׁנִּגְמַר דִּינוֹ שֶׁנִּתְעָרֵב בִּשְׁוָרִים אֲחֵרִים, סוֹקְלִים אוֹתָם, דְּהָא עַל כָּרְחֲךָ כֻּלְּהוּ אֲסוּרִים בַּהֲנָאָה וַאֲפִלּוּ הֵן אֶלֶף, מִפְּנֵי זֶה הַמְעֹרָב בָּהֶן, הִלְכָּךְ סוֹקְלִין אֶת כֻּלָּם כְּדֵי שֶׁתִּתְקַיֵּם מִצְוַת סְקִילָה בַּמְחֻיָּב בָּהּ. [רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר] כּוֹנְסִין אוֹתָן לְכִפָּה, וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לְסָקְלָן, אֶלָּא כוֹנְסִין אוֹתָן לְחֶדֶר וְהֵן מֵתִים בָּרָעָב. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה אומר כונסין אותן לכיפה – Our Mishnah is deficient and this is how it should be read as follows: An ox whose proceedings are finished (i.e., the sentence was pronounced) that it was mixed up with other oxen , they stone them, for perforce, all of them forbidden to derive benefit from them and even if they were one-thousand, because of this ox that was mixed up with others, therefore we stone all of them in order that the Mitzvah of stoning can be fulfilled with the one who was liable for it. [Rabbi Yehuda states]: They put them all in prison., and there is no need to stone them, but rather, put them in a room and they will die of hunger. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:3:3)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר בְּסַיִף.** שֶׁהַחֶנֶק חָמוּר:


ר' שמעון אומר בסייף – for choking is more stringent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:3:4)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים בְּחֶנֶק.** שֶׁהַסַּיִף חָמוּר:


וחכמים אומרים בחנק – for the sword is more stringent.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:4:1)


**מִי שֶׁנִּתְחַיֵּב שְׁתֵּי מִיתוֹת.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁעָבַר עֲבֵרָה קַלָּה וְנִגְמַר דִּינוֹ לַעֲבֵרָה קַלָּה, וְחָזַר וְעָבַר עֲבֵרָה חֲמוּרָה, סָלְקָא דַעְתָּךְ אֲמֵינָא כֵּיוָן דְּנִגְמַר דִּינוֹ לַעֲבֵרָה קַלָּה, הַאי גַּבְרָא קְטִילָא הוּא, קָמַשְׁמַע לָן:


מי שנתחייב שתי מיתות – such as the case where a person committed a minor transgression and when the proceedings were finished (i.e., the sentence was pronounced), he went and committed a major transgression; one might think that since the proceedings were finished for the minor transgression, that he is a man to be put to death, this comes to teach us that this is not the case.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:4:2)


**עָבַר עֲבֵרָה שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ שְׁתֵּי מִיתוֹת.** כְּגוֹן חֲמוֹתוֹ, וְהִיא אֵשֶׁת אִישׁ:


עבר עבירה ישי בה שתי מיתות – such as the case of his mother-in-law, and she is a married woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:4:3)


**נִדּוֹן בַּחֲמוּרָה.** בִּשְׂרֵפָה, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהִיא חֲמוֹתוֹ, וְלֹא כְאֵשֶׁת אִישׁ שֶׁהִיא בְחֶנֶק:


נידון בחמורה – with burning (i.e., swallowing a molten-liquid), because she is his mother-in-law, and not like [for a transgression] with a married woman which is [punishable] by choking.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:4:4)


**נִדּוֹן בַּזִּקָּה הָרִאשׁוֹנָה.** בְּאוֹתוֹ אִסּוּר שֶׁהֻזְקַק תְּחִלָּה לְהִזָּהֵר וְלִפְרֹשׁ מִמֶּנּוּ הוּא נִדּוֹן. אֲבָל לֹא בָאִסּוּר הַבָּא עָלָיו אַחֲרוֹן, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא חָמוּר, דְּקָסָבַר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אֵין אִסּוּר חָל עַל אִסּוּר, וַאֲפִלּוּ חָמוּר עַל הַקַּל. וְאִם נָשָׂא בַת אַלְמָנָה שֶׁהָיְתָה תְחִלָּה חֲמוֹתוֹ כְּשֶׁהָיְתָה פְנוּיָה וְאַחַר כָּךְ נִשֵּׂאת, נִדּוֹן בִּשְׂרֵפָה. וְאִם הָיְתָה אֵשֶׁת אִישׁ וְאַחַר כָּךְ נַעֲשֵׂית חֲמוֹתוֹ, נִדּוֹן בְּחֶנֶק כְּמִיתָה שֶׁל אֵשֶׁת אִישׁ שֶׁהָיְתָה תְחִלָּה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


נדון בזיקה הראשונה – with that prohibited act that he was obligated to regard first to be careful to separate himself from he is judged, but not on the prohibition that he comes upon last, even though it is the more stringent, for Rabbi Yosi holds that one prohibition does not take effect on another, and even the more stringent upon the lenient, and if he married the daughter of a widow, who was first his mother-in-law when she was a free-woman and after that she married, he is judged with burning (i.e., swallowing a molten-liquid). And if she was a married woman and afterwards became his mother-in-law, she is judged with choking, like the death of a married woman that she was at first. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yosi.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:5:1)


**מִי שֶׁלָּקָה וְשָׁנָה.** שֶׁלָּקָה שְׁתֵּי פְעָמִים עַל עֲבֵרָה שֶׁחַיָּבִים עָלֶיהָ כָרֵת, שֶׁכָּל חַיָּבֵי כְרֵתוֹת לוֹקִים. כְּשֶׁיַּחֲזֹר וְיַעֲשֶׂה אוֹתָהּ עֲבֵרָה עַצְמָהּ פַּעַם שְׁלִישִׁית:


מי שלקה ושנה – he was flogged twice on a transgression that he would be liable for extirpation, for all those who are liable for extirpation are flogged, and when he would come back and perform that same transgression a third time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:5:2)


**מַכְנִיסִין אוֹתוֹ לְכִפָּה.** מָקוֹם כְּשִׁעוּר קוֹמַת אָדָם וְלֹא יוֹתֵר. וּמַאֲכִילִין אוֹתוֹ תְחִלָּה לֶחֶם צַר וּמַיִם לַחַץ עַד שֶׁיֻּקְטְנוּ בְנֵי מֵעָיו, וַהֲדַר מַאֲכִילִין אוֹתוֹ שְׂעֹרִים שֶׁנּוֹפְחוֹת בְּמֵעָיו עַד שֶׁכְּרֵסוֹ נִבְקַעַת:


מכניסין אותו לכיפה – a place that is measured like the stature (i.e. height) of a person and not more, and they would feed him first the bread of adversity (i.e., scanty rations) and water of distress/affliction until his intestines shrink (Sanhedrin 81b), and afterwards they would feed him barley that would blow up his intestines until his belly explodes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:5:3)


**שֶׁלֹּא בְעֵדִים.** שֶׁלֹּא בְעֵדוּת שֶׁיִּהְיֶה מְחֻיָּב עָלֶיהָ מִיתָה. וּמִכָּל מָקוֹם יָדוּעַ לְבֵית דִּין שֶׁהַדָּבָר אֱמֶת שֶׁבְּוַדַּאי הָרַג, אֶלָּא שֶׁהֻכְחֲשׁוּ הָעֵדִים בִּבְדִיקוֹת אוֹ שֶׁלֹּא הָיְתָה שָׁם הַתְרָאָה מַסְפֶּקֶת:


שלא בעדים – for it is not through testimony that he will be liable for death. And nevertheless, it is known to the Jewish court that the matter is true and that he certainly killed, but the witnesses were contradicted in their examinations or that there was a doubtful warning.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:5:4)


**וּמַאֲכִילִין אוֹתוֹ לֶחֶם צַר וּמַיִם לָחַץ.** תְּחִלָּה, וְאַחַר כָּךְ מַאֲכִילִין אוֹתוֹ שְׂעֹרִים עַד שֶׁכְּרֵסוֹ נִבְקַעַת. וְהַיְנוּ רֵישָׁא. וּמַה שֶּׁחִסֵּר זֶה גִּלָּה זֶה:


ומאכילין אותו לחם צר ומים לחץ – first and afterwards we feed him barley until his stomach explodes, and that is the first part [of the Mishnah] and what this part was lacking, the other part revealed.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:6:1)


**הַגּוֹנֵב אֶת הַקַּסְוָה.** אֶחָד מִכְּלֵי שָׁרֵת. מִלְּשׁוֹן קְשׂוֹת הַנָּסֶךְ (במדבר ד):


הגונב את הקסוה – one of the service vessels (see Talmud Sanhedrin 81b), from the language (Numbers 4:7): “and the libation jugs.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:6:2)


**וְהַמְקַלֵּל בַּקּוֹסֵם.** הַמְבָרֵךְ אֶת הַשֵּׁם בְּשֵׁם עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה:


המקלל בקוסם – who blasphemes the name [of God] in the name of idolatry.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:6:3)


**וְהַבּוֹעֵל אֲרַמִּית.** נָכְרִית:


והבועל ארמית – a heathen woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:6:4)


**קַנָּאִים פּוֹגְעִים בּוֹ.** הַמְקַנְּאִים קִנְאָתוֹ שֶׁל מָקוֹם הָיוּ הוֹרְגִים אוֹתוֹ. וְהוּא שֶׁתִּהְיֶה הַנָּכְרִית בַּת נָכְרִית, וּבִשְׁעַת מַעֲשֶׂה, וּבִפְנֵי עֲשָׂרָה מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל. וְאִם חָסֵר אֶחָד מִן הַתְּנָאִים הַלָּלוּ אָסוּר לְהָרְגוֹ. אֲבָל עָנְשׁוֹ מְפֹרָשׁ עַל פִּי נָבִיא, יַכְרֵת ה'‏ לָאִישׁ אֲשֶׁר יַעֲשֶׂנָּה. וּמַלְקִין אוֹתוֹ אַרְבַּע מַלְקֻיּוֹת מִדִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים, מִשּׁוּם נִדָּה, מִשּׁוּם שִׁפְחָה מִשּׁוּם נָכְרִית, מִשּׁוּם זוֹנָה:


קנאים פוגעים בו – those who are zealous with the zealousness of God would kill him. And it would be a heathen woman the daughter of those who worship idols, and at the time of the act, and before ten [men] of Israel (i.e., the community) , and if one of these conditions is missing, it is prohibited to kill him. But, his punishment is explained by the prophet (Malachi 2:12): “May the LORD leave to him who does this no descendants [dwelling in the tents of Jacob and presenting offerings to the LORD of Hosts].” And we flog him four times from the words of the Scribes: because of her being a menstruant woman, because she is a maid-servant, because she is a heathen and because she is a harlot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:6:5)


**פִּרְחֵי כְהֻנָּה.** בַּחוּרִים שֶׁמַּתְחִיל שְׂעַר זְקָנָם לִפְרֹחַ בָּהֶם:


פרחי כהונה – boys whose hair of their beards begin to flower on them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:6:6)


**בִּגְזִירִין.** בְּקָעִיּוֹת שֶׁל עֵצִים:


בגזירין – logs of wood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:6:7)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר בְּחֶנֶק.** נֶאֱמַר כָּאן (במדבר א) וְהַזָּר הַקָּרֵב יוּמָת, וְנֶאֱמַר לְהַלָּן (דברים יג) וְהַנָּבִיא הַהוּא אוֹ חֹלֵם הַחֲלוֹם הַהוּא יוּמָת, מַה לְהַלָּן בְּחֶנֶק אַף כָּאן בְּחֶנֶק:


רבי עקיבא אמור בחנק – it is stated here (Numbers 1:51): “Any foreigner who encroaches (the Tabernacle) shall be put to death,” and it is stated there (Deuteronomy 13:6): “As for that prophet or dream-diviner, he shall be put to death…” Just as there, [his death] is by choking, so also here by choking.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 9:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/9:6:8)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים בִּידֵי שָׁמָיִם.** נֶאֱמַר כָּאן יוּמָת, וְנֶאֱמַר לְהַלָּן (במדבר יז) כָּל הַקָּרֵב הַקָּרֵב אֶל מִשְׁכַּן ה'‏ יָמוּת, מַה לְהַלָּן בִּידֵי שָׁמַיִם אַף כָּאן בִּידֵי שָׁמַיִם. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים. וּמִיתָה בִידֵי שָׁמַיִם הוּא פָחוֹת מִן הַכָּרֵת, שֶׁהַכָּרֵת יֵשׁ עָלָיו עֹנֶשׁ לְאַחַר מִיתָה אִם לֹא עָשָׂה תְשׁוּבָה כָרָאוּי, וּמִיתָה בִידֵי שָׁמַיִם אֵין לְאַחַר מִיתָה עָלָיו כְּלוּם. וְרַשִׁ"י כָתַב בְּפֶרֶק בַּמֶּה מַדְלִיקִין (שבת דף כה) דְּמִיתָה בִידֵי שָׁמַיִם יָמָיו נִקְצָרִים וְאֵינוֹ הוֹלֵךְ עֲרִירִי, כָּרֵת אִית בֵּיהּ תַּרְתֵּי, יָמָיו נִכְרָתִים וְהוֹלֵךְ עֲרִירִי. וּמְחֻיָּבֵי מִיתָה בִידֵי שָׁמַיִם שֶׁעַל עִסְקֵי מִקְדָּשׁ וְקָדָשִׁים הֵם אַחַד עָשָׂר, וְאֵלּוּ הֵן, הָאוֹכֵל טֶבֶל, וְכֹהֵן טָמֵא שֶׁאָכַל תְּרוּמָה טְהוֹרָה, וְזָר שֶׁאָכַל תְּרוּמָה, וְזָר וְטָמֵא וּטְבוּל יוֹם שֶׁשִּׁמְּשׁוּ, וּמְחֻסַּר כִּפּוּרִים, וּמְחֻסַּר בְּגָדִים, וְשֶׁלֹּא קִדֵּשׁ יָדַיִם וְרַגְלַיִם, וּשְׁתוּיֵי יַיִן, וּפְרוּעֵי רֹאשׁ. קְצָת מֵהֶם נֶאֱמַר מִיתָה בָהֶם בְּפֵרוּשׁ, וּקְצָתָם לְמָדוּם רַבּוֹתֵינוּ מִפִּי הַקַּבָּלָה בִּגְזֵרָה שָׁוָה וּבְהֶקֵּשׁ:


וחכמים אומרים בידי שמים – It is stated here, “he shall be put to death” (Numbers 1:51) and it is stated further on (Numbers 17:28): “Everyone who so as much as ventures near the LORD’s Tabernacle must die…” Just as further on, it as at the hands of Heaven, so here too at the hands of Heaven. And the Halakha is according to the Sages. But death at the hand of Heaven is less than from extirpation, for extirpation has upon it a punishment after death if he did not repent appropriately. But[regarding] death at the hands of Heaven, there is nothing after it at all. And Rashi wrote in the chapter “With what do we kindle [Shabbat lights]” (chapter two of Tractate Shabbat, 25a): that death at the hands of Heaven, his days are shortened and he does not go childless. Extirpation has two [things associated with it]: His days are cut off and he goes (i.e., dies) childless. And those who are liable to death at the hand of Heaven on the matters of the Sanctuary and the holy things are eleven, and they are: He who eats eatables forbidden pending the separation of sacred gifts, a ritually impure Kohen who eats pure priest’s due, and the foreigner (i.e., the non-Kohen) who eats priest’s due, and the foreigner, the impure and the ritual impurity of one who immersed during the daytime but does not attain ritual purity until the evening – who served [in the Sanctuary] , and those [Kohanim] lacking atonement, those [Kohanim] lacking priestly garments, and who did not sanctify their hands and feet and those who are intoxicated with wine and had overgrown hair. Some of them it says [the punishment of] death regarding them explicitly and some of them our Rabbis learned from the received tradition by verbal analogy and by juxtaposition.



## Chapter 10


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:1:1)


**כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל יֵשׁ לָהֶם חֵלֶק לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא.** אֲפִלּוּ אֵלּוּ שֶׁנִּתְחַיְּבוּ מִיתָה בְּבֵית דִּין מִפְּנֵי רִשְׁעָתָן, יֵשׁ לָהֶם חֵלֶק לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא. וְעוֹלָם הַבָּא הָאָמוּר כָּאן הוּא הָעוֹלָם הַבָּא אַחַר תְּחִיַּת הַמֵּתִים, שֶׁעֲתִידִים לִחְיוֹת וְלַעֲמֹד בְּגוּפָם וּבְנַפְשָׁם חַיִּים נִצְחִיִּים, כַּחַמָּה וְכַלְּבָנָה וְכַכּוֹכָבִים, כִּדְמַיְתֵי בַגְּמָרָא בְּהַאי פִּרְקָא, מֵתִים שֶׁעֲתִידִים לִחְיוֹת אֵין חוֹזְרִין לַעֲפָרָן. וְהָעוֹלָם הַבָּא אֵין בּוֹ לֹא אֲכִילָה וְלֹא שְׁתִיָּה, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ גוּף וּגְוִיָּה, אֶלָּא צַדִּיקִים יוֹשְׁבִין וְעַטְרוֹתֵיהֶן בְּרָאשֵׁיהֶן וְנֶהֱנִין מִזִּיו הַשְּׁכִינָה. וּמִפְּנֵי שֶׁאֵין כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל שָׁוִין בּוֹ, אֶלָּא הַגָּדוֹל לְפִי גָּדְלוֹ וְהַקָּטָן לִפְנֵי קָטְנוֹ, מִשּׁוּם הָכִי קָתָנֵי יֵשׁ לָהֶם חֵלֶק:


כל ישראל יש להם חלק לעולם הבא – even those who were convicted for death by the Jewish court because of their wickedness, they have a share in the World to Come. And the World to Come spoken of here is the World to Come after the Resurrection of the Dead, who would be destined to live and to stand with their bodies and souls living eternally, like the sun, and the moon and the stars, as is brought in the Gemara in this chapter (Sanhedrin 90b), the dead who are destined to live do not return to the dust. And in the World to Come, there is no eating or drinking, and even though it has a body and inner-body (i.e., membrum), but the Righteous sit and their crowns are on their heads and they enjoy the splendor of God’s presence. And because all Israel is not equivalent in this, but rather the great according to their greatness and the small before his smallness. Because of this, it is taught that they have a share [in the World to Come].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:1:2)


**הָאוֹמֵר אֵין תְּחִיַּת הַמֵּתִים מִן הַתּוֹרָה.** בַּגְּמָרָא קָאָמַר, וְכָל כָּךְ לָמָּה, תָּנָא, הוּא כָפַר בִּתְחִיַּת הַמֵּתִים, לְפִיכָךְ לֹא יִהְיֶה לוֹ חֵלֶק בִּתְחִיַּת הַמֵּתִים. וּמִכָּאן אֲנִי מוֹכִיחַ שֶׁעוֹלָם הַבָּא הָאָמוּר בְּמִשְׁנָה זוֹ אֵינוֹ הָעוֹלָם שֶׁהַנְּשָׁמוֹת מֻנָּחוֹת בּוֹ בַּזְּמַן הַזֶּה, אֶלָּא הָעוֹלָם שֶׁל תְּחִיַּת הַמֵּתִים כְּדִפְרֵשִׁית:


האומר אין תחיית המתים מן התורה – In the Gemara (Sanhedrin 90a) it states, and so much why, as it is taught: he denied the Resurrection of the Dead. Therefore, he will not have a share in the Resurrection of the Dead. And from here, I prove that the World to Come that is mentioned in this Mishnah is not the world in which the souls are placed at this time, but rather, the world of the Resurrection of the Dead as I have mentioned.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:1:3)


**אֶפִּיקוֹרֶס.** שֶׁמְּבַזֶּה תַלְמִידֵי חֲכָמִים. וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן הַמְבַזֶּה הַתּוֹרָה עַצְמָהּ:


אפיקורוס – that despises the Sages and all the more despises the Torah itself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:1:4)


**בַּסְּפָרִים הַחִיצוֹנִים.** סִפְרֵי מִינִים, כְּגוֹן סִפְרֵי אַרִיסְט"וֹ הַיְוָנִי וַחֲבֵרָיו. וּבִכְלַל זֶה הַקּוֹרֵא בְסֵפֶר דִּבְרֵי הַיָּמִים שֶׁל מַלְכֵי נָכְרִים וּבְשִׁירִים שֶׁל עֲגָבִים וְדִבְרֵי חֵשֶׁק שֶׁאֵין בָּהֶם חָכְמָה וְלֹא תוֹעֶלֶת אֶלָּא אִבּוּד זְמַן בִּלְבָד:


בספרים החיצונים – the books of the sectarians, such as the books of Aristotle the Greek and his colleagues. And included in this – a person who reads the chronicles of the heathen kings, “songs of sensuality” [=erotic poems], and “writings of desire” [=novels] that have no wisdom in them or purpose other than a waste of time alone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:1:5)


**וְהַלּוֹחֵשׁ עַל הַמַּכָּה.** וּבְרוֹקֵק בִּלְבָד הוּא דְאֵין לוֹ חֵלֶק לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין מַזְכִּירִין שֵׁם שָׁמַיִם עַל הָרְקִיקָה:


והלוחש על המכה – and with spitting alone that he has not share in the World to Come, for he does not mention the name of Heaven on the spitting (Sanhedrin 101a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:1:6)


**וְהַהוֹגֶה אֶת הַשֵּׁם.** שֶׁל אַרְבַּע אוֹתִיּוֹת כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא נִכְתָּב:


וההוגה את השם – of four letters like it is written.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:2:1)


**שְׁלֹשָׁה מְלָכִים וְאַרְבָּעָה הֶדְיוֹטוֹת.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁגְּדוֹלִים וַחֲכָמִים הָיוּ, אֵין לָהֶם חֵלֶק לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא.


שלשה מלכים וארבעה הדיוטות – even though they were great men and Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:2:2)


**שֶׁלֹּא הָיְתָה אֱמוּנָתָם שְׁלֵמָה.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּבִלְעָם מֵאֻמּוֹת הָעוֹלָם הֲוָה, וַאֲנַן כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל יֵשׁ לָהֶם חֵלֶק לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא תְּנַן, מִשּׁוּם דְּקַיְמָא לָן חֲסִידֵי אֻמּוֹת הָעוֹלָם יֵשׁ לָהֶם חֵלֶק לָעוֹלָם הַבָּא, אַשְׁמְעִינַן דְּבִלְעָם לָאו מֵחֲסִידֵי אֻמּוֹת הָעוֹלָם הוּא:


אין להם חלק לעוה"ב – for their belief was not complete. And even though that Balaam was from the nations of the world, and it is taught [in Mishnah 1], "all Israel have a share in the world to come", because we hold that the righteous of the nations of the world have a share in the world to come, [the Mishnah] comes to teach us that Balaam was not from the righteous of the nations of the world.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:3:1)


**שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר לֹא יָדוֹן רוּחִי בָאָדָם, לֹא דִין וְלֹא רוּחַ.** שֶׁאֵין עוֹמְדִים בַּדִּין וְאֵין לָהֶם רוּחַ לִחְיוֹת עִם הַצַּדִּיקִים שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶם חֵלֶק:


שנאמר לא ידון רוחי באדם לא דין ולא רוח – for they do not stand in judgment and they have no spirit to live with the righteous that have a share.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:3:2)


**כֹּרְתֵי בְרִיתִי עֲלֵי זָבַח.** שֶׁכָּרְתוּ בְרִית עִם הַמָּקוֹם עֲלֵי זְבָחִים וּשְׁלָמִים, דִּכְתִיב (שמות כד) וַיִּזְבְּחוּ זְבָחִים שְׁלָמִים, וּכְתִיב (שם) וַיִּזְרֹק עַל הָעָם וַיֹּאמֶר הִנֵּה דַם הַבְּרִית:


כורתי בריתי עלי זבח – for they made a covenant with God over sacrifice and peace offerings, as it is written (Exodus 24:5): “[He designated some young men among the Israelites,’ and they offered burnt offerings and [sacrificed bulls] as offerings of well-being [to the LORD],” and it is written (Exodus 24:8): “[Moses took the blood] and dashed it on the people and said, ‘This the blood of the covenant [that the LORD now makes with you concerning all these commands].’”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:3:3)


**אֵין עֲתִידִים לַחֲזֹר.** מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁגָּלוּ. וְהָא דְאָמְרִינַן שֶׁיִּרְמְיָה הֶחֱזִירָן וְיֹאשִׁיָּהוּ בֶן אָמוֹן מָלַךְ עֲלֵיהֶם, לֹא כֻלָּן חָזְרוּ אֶלָּא מִקְצָתָן:


אין עתידים לחזור – from the place where they were exiled. For we have said that Jeremiah brought them back and Josiah the son of Amon ruled over them, for not all of them came back, but rather part of them.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:4:1)


**יָצְאוּ אֲנָשִׁים בְּנֵי בְלִיַּעַל.** בָּנִים שֶׁאֵינָם עוֹלִים בִּתְחִיַּת הַמֵּתִים, וְנִדָּחִין כַּמַּדִּיחִין:


יצאו אנשים בני בליעל – children that do not rise up at the [time of the] Resurrection of the Dead. And those who are led astray are like those who lead astray.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:4:2)


**עַד שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מַדִּיחֶיהָ מֵאוֹתָהּ הָעִיר.** דִּכְתִיב וַיַּדִּיחוּ אֶת יֹשְׁבֵי עִירָם, וְלֹא יוֹשְׁבֵי עִיר אַחֶרֶת:


עד שיהיו מדיחים מאותה העיר – as it is written (Deuteronomy 13:14): “…and subverted the inhabitants of their town…,” but not the inhabitants of another town.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:4:3)


**וּמֵאוֹתוֹ הַשֵּׁבֶט.** דִּכְתִיב מִקִּרְבֶּךָ, מִקֶּרֶב הַשֵּׁבֶט עַצְמוֹ:


ומאותו השבט – as it is written (Deuteronomy 13:14): “[that some scoundrels] from among you…” from the midst of that tribe itself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:4:4)


**וְעַד שֶׁיֻּדַּח רֻבָּהּ.** שֶׁל עִיר. דִּכְתִיב יוֹשְׁבֵי עִירָם, דְּמַשְׁמַע יִשּׁוּבָהּ שֶׁל עִיר דְּהַיְנוּ רֻבָּהּ:


ועד שיודח רובה – of the city, as it is written (Deuteronomy 13:14): “of their town” – which implies the settlement of that city, which means the majority.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:4:5)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ כִיחִידִים.** שֶׁעָבְדוּ עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה. וְיִדּוֹנוּ בִסְקִילָה וּמָמוֹנָן פָּלֵט:


הרי אלו כיחידים – as individuals who served idolatry and are judged with stoning and their property is spared.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:4:6)


**וּצְרִיכִים.** אַנְשֵׁי עִיר הַנִּדַּחַת:


וצריכים – the men of the Apostate City.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:4:7)


**שְׁנֵי עֵדִים וְהַתְרָאָה לְכָל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד.** שֶׁהָיוּ מַרְבִּים לָהֶם בָּתֵּי דִינִין, וְכָל מִי שֶׁנִּמְצָא שֶׁעָבַד עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה בְּעֵדִים וְהַתְרָאָה מַפְרִישִׁים אוֹתָן עַד שֶׁיִּרְאוּ אִם הֵם רֻבָּהּ שֶׁל עִיר, מְבִיאִין אוֹתָן לְבֵית דִּין הַגָּדוֹל וְגוֹמְרִין דִּינָן שָׁם, וְהוֹרְגִים אוֹתָם בְּסַיִף וּמָמוֹנָן אָבֵד. וְאִם לֹא נִמְצְאוּ רֻבָּהּ שֶׁל עִיר, דָּנִין אוֹתָן בִּסְקִילָה וּמָמוֹנָן פָּלֵט:


שני עדים והתראה לכל אחד ואחד – for they had increased the number of courts, and every person who was found to have worshipped idolatry with witnesses and warning, we separate them until they see if they are a majority of the city, we would bring them to the Great Jewish Court and complete their judgment there and kill them with the sword and their property would be lost. But if they did not find the majority of [the residents of] the city, we would judge them with stoning and their property would be spared.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:5:1)


**הַחַמֶּרֶת וְהַגַּמֶּלֶת.** שַׁיָּרָא שֶׁל חַמָּרִים וְשֶׁל גַּמָּלִים שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּהוּ בָעִיר שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם, הָוֵי לְהוּ בִכְלַל יוֹשְׁבֵי עִירָם:


החמרת והגמלת – a caravan of donkeys and of camels that were delayed in the city for thirty days, they are included in those who are the dwellers of their city.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:5:2)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ מַצִּילִין.** אִם רֹב אַנְשֵׁי הָעִיר הֻדְּחוּ וּמִעוּט לֹא הֻדְּחוּ, וְחַמָּרִים וְגַמָּלִים שֶׁלֹּא הֻדְּחוּ מַשְׁלִימִין אֶת הַמֻּעָט לַעֲשׂוֹתָן רֹב, הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ מַצִּילִין, שֶׁאֵין מָמוֹנָן אָבֵד אֶלָּא נִדּוֹנִין כִּיחִידִים. וְהוּא הַדִּין דְּגוֹרְמִין נַמִּי לַעֲשׂוֹת עִיר הַנִּדַּחַת, אִם הֻדְּחוּ עִמָּם לַעֲשׂוֹתָם רֹב, אֶלָּא דְתַנָּא אַזְּכוּתָא קָא מְהַדַּר. וְעוֹד דְּהָא פְּסִיקָא לֵיהּ טְפֵי, דִּסְתַם חַמֶּרֶת וְגַמֶּלֶת אֵין דַּעְתָּן מְעֹרֶבֶת עִם בְּנֵי הָעִיר שֶׁיִּהְיוּ נִדָּחִין עִמָּהֶן:


הרי אלו מצילין – If most of the people of the city were led astray and a minority were not led astray, and [a caravan of] ass- and camel-drivers which were not led astray overturned the minority to become a majority, they save [the city] – thus the property is spared, and [the sinners] are judged as individuals.
