And the same is true that [a caravan] may cause [a city] to be rendered as an Apostate City if they were subverted with them to form a majority, however, the tanna is searching for [scenarios of] acquittal. Furthermore, [the tanna considered] this [scenario] more decisive, as typically, a caravan of ass- and camel-drivers is not well disposed towards the people of the city to be led astray with them.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:1)


**הָיָה רְחוֹבָהּ חוּצָה לָהּ.** אִם מְקוֹם קִבּוּץ בְּנֵי הָעִיר הָיָה חוּצָה לָהּ, כּוֹנְסִים אוֹתָהּ לְתוֹךְ הָעִיר, שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לַעֲשׂוֹת לָהּ רְחוֹב בְּתוֹכָהּ:


היתה רחובה חוצה לה – if the place of gathering the people of the city is outside of it, we bring them together into the city, for it is necessary to make for it a road inside it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:2)


**רְחוֹב.** פְּלַטְיָא גְדוֹלָה:


רחוב – a large thoroughfare.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:3)


**הֶקְדֵּשׁוֹת שֶׁבְּתוֹכָהּ יִפָּדוּ.** כְּלוֹמַר אֵין נִשְׂרָפִים אֶלָּא צְרִיכִים פְּדִיָּה כִּשְׁאָר כָּל הַהֶקְדֵּשׁוֹת:


הקדשות שבתוכה יפדו – that is to say, that they are not burned but the rquire redemption like the rest of the holy/dedicated objects.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:4)


**וּתְרוּמוֹת יֵרָקְבוּ.** בַּגְּמָרָא מוֹקְמִינַן לַהּ בִּתְרוּמָה בְיַד כֹּהֵן, דְּמָמוֹן כֹּהֵן הוּא וְחָל עֲלֵיהּ אִסּוּר דְּעִיר הַנִּדַּחַת. וּמִיהוּ לֹא הָיוּ הַתְּרוּמוֹת נִשְׂרָפִים כִּשְׁאָר שְׁלָלָהּ, דְּלֹא מְזַלְזְלִינַן בָּהּ כֻּלֵּי הַאי, לְפִיכָךְ יֵרָקְבוּ. וְאִם תְּרוּמָה בְיַד יִשְׂרָאֵל הִיא, שְׁלַל גָּבוֹהַּ הוּא וְתִנָּתֵן לְכֹהֵן שֶׁבְּעִיר אַחֶרֶת:


ותרומות ירקבו – In the Gemara (Sanhedrin 112b) we establish it concerning priest’s due in the hands of a Kohen, for it is the money of the Kohen and a prohibition of the Apostate City takes effect upon it. However, the priest’s due is not burned like the rest of its booty, for we do not denigrate it all that much. Therefore, it should decay/rot, and if the priest’s due is in the hands of an Israelite, it is the booty of “On High” (i.e., God) and should be given to a Kohen that is in another city.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:5)


**וּמַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּמָמוֹן יִשְׂרָאֵל הוּא שֶׁהֲרֵי הוּא נֶאֱכָל לְיִשְׂרָאֵל, הוֹאִיל וְקֹדֶשׁ אִקְּרֵי, לֹא יִשָּׂרֵף אֶלָּא יִגָּנֵז:


ומעשר שני – even though it is the money of an Israelite, for it is eaten by an Israelite, since it is called, “holy” (see Deuteronomy 26:13), it should not be burned, but rather hidden away.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:6)


**כָּלִיל לַה'‏ אֱלֹהֶיךָ.** אָמַר רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן וְכוּ'‏ גָּרְסִינַן. וְלֹא גָרְסִינַן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר כָּלִיל לַה'‏ אֱלֹהֶיךָ:


כליל לה' אלהיך – Rabbi Shimon said, we read [in our Mishnah] and we don’t read: “as it states, “every whit unto the LORD your God” (Deuteronomy 13:17).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:7)


**לֹא תֵעָשֶׂה גַנּוֹת וּפַרְדֵּסִים.** דְעוֹד, לְגַמְרֵי מַשְׁמַע:


לא תיעשה גנות ופרדסים – that furthermore, completely, is implied.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:8)


**לִכְמוֹ שֶׁהָיְתָה.** בְּיִשּׁוּב בָּתִּים:


כמו שהיתה – with the settlement in houses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 10:6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/10:6:9)


**אֲבָל נַעֲשֵׂית הִיא גַּנּוֹת וּפַרְדֵּסִים.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


אבל נעשית היא גנות ופרדסים – And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva.



## Chapter 11


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:1)


**אֵלּוּ הֵן הַנֶּחֱנָקִין.** מַמְרֵא עַל פִּי בֵית דִּין. שֶׁמְּסָרֵב עַל דִּבְרֵי בֵית דִּין הַגָּדוֹל שֶׁבְּלִשְׁכַּת הַגָּזִית:


אלו הן הנחנקין. ממרא על פי בית דין – he rebels against the words of the Great Jewish court that was in the Chamber of the Hewn Stones.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:2)


**וְזוֹמְמֵי בַת כֹּהֵן.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵם בָּאִים לְחַיְּבָהּ שְׂרֵפָה, אֵין נִדּוֹנִים אֶלָּא בַמִּיתָה שֶׁהָיוּ מְחַיְּבִים אֶת בּוֹעֲלָהּ שֶׁהוּא בְחֶנֶק, כִּשְׁאָר הַבָּא עַל אֵשֶׁת אִישׁ, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כא) הִיא בָּאֵשׁ תִּשָּׂרֵף, הִיא וְלֹא בוֹעֲלָהּ. וְזוֹמְמֶיהָ יָלְפִינַן מִכַּאֲשֶׁר זָמַם לַעֲשׂוֹת לְאָחִיו, וְלֹא לַאֲחוֹתוֹ:


זוממי בת כהן – even though they come to make her liable for [the punishment] of burning [by drinking a molten-liquid], they are not judged other than through the death that they were making liable those who engaged in sexual intercourse with her, which is by strangulation, like the others who come upon a married woman, as it is written (Leviticus 21:9): “she shall be put to the fire,” she and not the person who engaged in sexual intercourse with her, and those who falsely testified [through scheming] against her, we derive from (Deuteronomy 19:19): “[You shall do to him] as he schemed to do to his fellow,” but not to his sister.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:3)


**וּבוֹעֲלָהּ.** לְבַת כֹּהֵן כְּשֶׁהִיא נְשׂוּאָה. אֲבָל אֲרוּסָה, הִיא וּבוֹעֲלָהּ בִּסְקִילָה:


ובועלה – the daughter of a Kohen when she is married, but an engaged one, he and her sexual partner [are punished] by stoning.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:4)


**שֶׁהַמְקַלֵּל לְאַחַר מִיתָה חַיָּב.** דִּכְתִיב (שם כ) אָבִיו וְאִמּוֹ קִלֵּל, וּקְרָא יְתֵרָא הוּא לְרַבּוֹת לְאַחַר מִיתָה:


שהמקלל לאחר מיתה חייב – as it is written (Leviticus 20:9): “he has insulted his father and his mother. [His bloodguilt is upon him].” And the extra verse is to include after death.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:5)


**וְהַמַּכֶּה לְאַחַר מִיתָה פָּטוּר.** דְּהָא לֹא מִחַיַּב עַד שֶׁיַּעֲשֶׂה בוֹ חַבּוּרָה, וְאֵין חַבּוּרָה לְאַחַר מִיתָה:


והמכה לאחר מיתה פטור – for he is not liable until he makes in him a wound, but there is no wound after death.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:6)


**עַד שֶׁיַּכְנִיסֶנּוּ לִרְשׁוּתוֹ.** דִּכְתִיב (שמות כא) וְנִמְצָא בְיָדוֹ, וְאֵין יָדוֹ אֶלָּא רְשׁוּתוֹ, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר (במדבר כא) וַיִּקַּח אֶת כָּל אַרְצוֹ מִיָּדוֹ:


עד שיכניסנו לרשותו – as it is written (Exodus 21:16): “or if he be found in his hand”, and “his hand” means his domain. And similarly, it says (Numbers 21:26): “and taken all his land out of his hand,…”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:7)


**וְיִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בּוֹ.** תַּשְׁמִישׁ שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ שָׁוֶה פְרוּטָה. וְתַנָּא קַמָּא מְחַיֵּב אֲפִלּוּ בְתַשְׁמִישׁ שֶׁהוּא פָחוֹת מִשָּׁוֶה פְרוּטָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא:


וישתמש בו – usage that has the value of the equivalent of a Perutah/penny. And the first Tanna obligates him even with usage tha tis less than the equivalent of a penny. And the Halakha is according to the First Tanna.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:8)


**הַגּוֹנֵב אֶת בְּנוֹ.** טַעֲמַיְהוּ דְרַבָּנָן דְּפָטְרֵי, דִּכְתִיב וְנִמְצָא בְיָדוֹ, וּקְרָא יְתֵרָא הוּא, דְּהָא כְתִיב (דברים כד) כִּי יִמָּצֵא אִישׁ, אֶלָּא לְמִדְרַשׁ מִנַּהּ פְּרָט לָזֶה שֶׁהוּא מָצוּי. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


הגונב את בנו – the reason that the Rabbis exempt him, as it is written (Exodus 21:16): “or is still holding him,” and it is an extra verse, for it is written (Deuteronomy 24:7): “If a man is found [to have kidnapped a fellow Israelite…]”, to extrapolate from it, and exclude this one, who is already in his power. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:1:9)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה מְחַיֵּב.** דִּכְתִיב (שם) מֵאֶחָיו מִבְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל, מֵאֶחָיו לִמְעוּטֵי עֲבָדִים, מִבְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל, אִי כָתַב בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל הֲוָה מְמַעֲטִינַן מִי שֶׁחֶצְיוֹ עֶבֶד וְחֶצְיוֹ בֶּן חוֹרִין, הַשְׁתָּא דִכְתִיב מִבְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל, מִעוּטָא אַחֲרִינָא, וְאֵין מִעוּט אַחַר מִעוּט אֶלָּא לְרַבּוֹת. וְרַבָּנָן סָבְרֵי, מֵאֶחָיו לָאו לִמְעוּטֵי עֲבָדִים, דְּהָא אַחִים הֵם בַּמִּצְוֹת, אֶלָּא בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לִמְעוּטֵי עֲבָדִים, מִבְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לִמְעוּטֵי מִי שֶׁחֶצְיוֹ עֶבֶד וְחֶצְיוֹ בֶן חוֹרִין. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


רבי יהודה מחייב – as it is written (Deuteronomy 24:7): “of his brethren of the children of Israel” – “of his brethren”, to exclude slaves, “of the children of Israel” – if it had written “of Israel”, we would have excluded someone who is half a servant and half a free-man. Now that it is written, from “of the children of Israel”, this is another exclusion, and where there is a double exclusion it indicates an exemplification (see Bava Kamma 86b). But the Rabbis hold, that “of his brethren” does not come to exclude slaves, for they are “brothers” in the commandments. Rather, the words “the children of Israel” excludes slaves; “of [the children of Israel]” excludes someone who is half-slave and half a free-man (see Mishnah Eduyot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 13 and Mishnah Gittin 4:5 which deals specifically with the case of someone who is a half-slave and half a free-man). And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:2:1)


**שְׁלֹשָׁה בָתֵּי דִינִין הָיוּ שָׁם.** בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, דְּמִשְׁתַּעֵי בָהּ קְרָא וְקַמְתָּ וְעָלִיתָ:


שלשה בתי דינין היו שם – In Jerusalem that Scripture is talking about (Deuteronomy 17:8): “you shall promptly repair [to the place that the LORD your God will have chosen…]”.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:2:2)


**אֶחָד יוֹשֵׁב עַל פֶּתַח הַר הַבַּיִת.** הוּא שַׁעַר מִזְרָחִי שֶׁלִּפְנִים מִן הַחֵיל, לִפְנֵי עֶזְרַת נָשִׁים:


אחד יושב על פתח הר הבית – it is the eastern gate that is inside from the חיל /the place within the fortification of the Temple, in front of the women’s court.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:2:3)


**וְאֶחָד יוֹשֵׁב.** לְמַעְלָה הֵימֶנּוּ, כְּשֶׁעָבְרוּ עֶזְרַת נָשִׁים וּבָאִים לְפֶתַח עֶזְרַת יִשְׂרָאֵל:


ואחד יושב – above him, when they have passed the women’s court and come to the door of the Israelite court.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:2:4)


**וְאֶחָד יוֹשֵׁב בְּלִשְׁכַּת הַגָּזִית.** שֶׁהִיא בְנוּיָה בְתוֹךְ הָעֲזָרָה חֶצְיָהּ בַּקֹּדֶשׁ וְחֶצְיָהּ בַּחֹל:


ואחד בלשכת הגזית – which is built in the midst of the court, half of it in sanctified [area] and half of it in non-sacred [area].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:2:5)


**בָּאִים לָזֶה שֶׁעַל פֶּתַח הַר הַבַּיִת.** זָקֵן זֶה שֶׁהוֹרָה בְעִירוֹ וְנֶחְלְקוּ בֵית דִּין שֶׁבְּעִירוֹ עָלָיו וְהִזְקִיקָן הַכָּתוּב לַעֲלוֹת לִירוּשָׁלַיִם, בָּאִים הוּא וּבֵית דִּין שֶׁבְּעִירוֹ לָזֶה בֵית דִּין שֶׁעַל פֶּתַח הַר הַבַּיִת, שֶׁהֲרֵי בוֹ פוֹגְעִים תְּחִלָּה:


באים לזה שעל פתח הר הבית – this elder who taught in his city, and the Jewish court that was in his city disputed with him and the Written [Torah] requires him to go up to Jerusalem. They come – he and the Jewish court that is in his city to this Jewish court at the entrance of the Temple Mount, for they first make contact with him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:2:6)


**תַּלְמִיד שֶׁהוֹרָה לַעֲשׂוֹת.** תַּלְמִיד שֶׁלֹּא הִגִּיעַ לְהוֹרָאָה וְנֶחֱלַק עַל בֵּית דִּין שֶׁבְּעִירוֹ וּבָאוּ לְבֵית דִּין הַגָּדוֹל וְשָׁאֲלוּ וְחָזַר לְעִירוֹ וְהוֹרָה כְבַתְּחִלָּה, פָּטוּר, שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם לִסְמֹךְ עַל הוֹרָאָתוֹ, וְהַתּוֹרָה לֹא חִיְּבָה אֶלָּא מֻפְלָא וּמֻמְחֶה לְבֵית דִּין, כִּדְיָלְפִינַן מִכִּי יִפָּלֵא מִמְּךָ, בַּמֻּפְלָא שֶׁבְּבֵית דִּין הַכָּתוּב מְדַבֵּר:


תלמיד שהורה לעשות – a student who had not arrived at being an authorized teacher, and disputed with the Jewish court that was in his city and they came to the Great Jewish court and asked and he returned to his city and he taught as at first, he is exempt, as they don’t have to rely upon his teaching, and the Torah did not make liable other than someone who is distinguished and expert to the Jewish court, as we derive it (Deuteronomy 17:8): “If a case is too baffling for you…” with the distinguished one of the Jewish court, Scripture speaks.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:2:7)


**נִמְצָא חֻמְרוֹ קֻלּוֹ.** חֹמֶר הָעֲבֵרָה שֶׁעָבַר וְהוֹרָה אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא הִגִּיעַ לְהוֹרָאָה מוּסָף עַל הַהַמְרָאָה שֶׁהִמְרָה עַל פִּי בֵית דִּין, הִיא נַעֲשֵׂית לוֹ קֻלָּא לְפָטְרוֹ מִן הַמִּיתָה, שֶׁאִם הָיָה זָקֵן שֶׁהִגִּיעַ לְהוֹרָאָה וְהִמְרָה עַל פִּי בֵית דִּין הָיָה חַיָּב מִיתָה:


נמצא חומרו קולו – the stringency of the transgression that he committed and he taught, even though he had not yet arrived at being an authorized teacher, is added to the contempt of court (i.e., rebelliousness) that he disregarded the authority of the Supreme Court, and it became for him a leniency to exempt him from death, for if he had been an elder who had arrived at being an authorized teacher, and disregarded the authority of the Supreme Court, he would be liable to death.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:3:1)


**הָאוֹמֵר אֵין תְּפִלִּין וְכוּ'‏ פָּטוּר.** שֶׁאֵין זוֹ הוֹרָאָה. דְּזִיל קְרֵי בֵי רַב הוּא:


האומר אין תפילין, וכו' פ טור – this is not instruction, for [it is a case of] “go, call the school of the Rabbi.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:3:2)


**חָמֵשׁ טוֹטָפוֹת.** יֵשׁ הוֹרָאָה. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין דָּבָר זֶה אֶלָּא לְהוֹסִיף עַל דִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים, חַיָּב, שֶׁבְּמִדְרַשׁ סוֹפְרִים [לְטֹטָפֹת לְטֹטֶפֶת] לְטוֹטָפֹת כְּתִיב, הֲרֵי כָאן אַרְבַּע פָּרָשִׁיּוֹת:


חמש טוטפות – there is instruction. For event though this matter [only comes] to add to the words of the Scribes, he is liable, for in the Midrash of the scribes, it is written,[ "לטטפת" /L’Totafat – as a frontlet (see Exodus 13:16); "לטטפת"/L’Totafat – as a frontlet] (see Deuteronomy 6:8) (the first two are written as singular words; the last one is a plural construction); "לטוטפת" /L’Totafot – as frontlets (see Deuteronomy 11:18), here we have four portions.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:4:1)


**וְלֹא בְבֵית דִּין שֶׁבְּיַבְנֶה.** שָׁאֲלוּ לְבֵית דִּין שֶׁבְּלִשְׁכַּת הַגָּזִית וְאָמְרוּ לוֹ, וְחָזַר לְעִירוֹ וְשָׁהָה שָׁם יָמִים רַבִּים עַד שֶׁגָּלְתָה סַנְהֶדְרִי גְדוֹלָה לְיַבְנֶה וְאַחַר כָּךְ הוֹרָה כְבַתְּחִלָּה, אֵין מְמִיתִין אוֹתוֹ בְיַבְנֶה, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁסַּנְהֶדְרֵי גְדוֹלָה שָׁם, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעֲדַיִן הַבַּיִת קַיָּם, כֵּיוָן שֶׁסַּנְהֶדְרֵי גְדוֹלָה אֵינָהּ יוֹשֶׁבֶת בִּמְקוֹמָהּ בְּלִשְׁכַּת הַגָּזִית:


ולא בב"ד שביבנה – for they asked the Jewish court that was in the Chamber of the Hewn Stones, and they said to him and he returned to his city and delayed there for many days until the Great Sanhedrin was exiled to Yavneh, and afterwards, it taught as at first, they do not put him to death in Yavneh, even though the Great Sanhedrin was there, and even if the Temple still existed, since the Great Sanhedrin does not sit in its place in the Chamber of the Hewn Stones.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:5:1)


**מַה שֶּׁלֹּא שָׁמַע.** מַה שֶּׁלֹּא נֶאֱמַר בִּנְבוּאָה לְשׁוּם נָבִיא בָעוֹלָם. וּמַה שֶּׁנֶּאֱמַר לַחֲבֵרוֹ וְלֹא לוֹ, וּשְׁמָעָהּ מִפִּי חֲבֵרוֹ וּבָא וְאָמַר שֶׁנֶּאֶמְרָה לוֹ, שְׁנֵי אֵלּוּ נְבִיאֵי הַשֶּׁקֶר הֵם וּמִיתָתָן בְּחֶנֶק. דִּכְתִיב (דברים יח) אַךְ הַנָּבִיא אֲשֶׁר יָזִיד לְדַבֵּר דָּבָר בִּשְׁמִי, זֶה הַמִּתְנַבֵּא מַה שֶּׁלֹּא שָׁמַע מֵעוֹלָם. אֲשֶׁר לֹא צִוִּיתִיו, הָא לַחֲבֵרוֹ צִוִּיתִי, זֶה הַמִּתְנַבֵּא מַה שֶּׁלֹּא נֶאֱמַר לוֹ וְנֶאֱמַר לַחֲבֵרוֹ. וּכְתִיב וּמֵת הַנָּבִיא הַהוּא, וְכָל מִיתָה הָאֲמוּרָה בַתּוֹרָה סְתָם אֵינָהּ אֶלָּא חֶנֶק:


מה שלא שמע – what was not said in the prophecy to a single prophet in the world, and what was said to his fellow and not to him, and he heard it from his fellow and came and said what was related to him, both of these two are false prophets and their deaths are by strangulation, as it is written (Deuteronomy 18:20): “But any prophet who presumes to speak in My name [an oracle that I did not command him to utter…]” This is one who prophesizes something that he never heard, “that I did not command him to utter,” but his friend I commanded. The one who prophesizes what was not said to him and it was said to his fellow, and it is written (ibid.) “that prophet shall die.” And any death that is mentioned in the Torah undefined is not other than through strangulation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:5:2)


**מִיתָתָן בִּידֵי שָׁמַיִם.** דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְהָיָה הָאִישׁ אֲשֶׁר לֹא יִשְׁמַע אֶל דְּבָרַי, קָרֵי בֵיהּ לֹא יִשְׁמַע, וּקְרִי בֵיהּ לֹא יַשְׁמִיעַ, וּקְרִי בֵיהּ לֹא יִשָּׁמַע הוּא עַצְמוֹ אֶל דְּבָרַי, הֲרֵי שְׁלָשְׁתָּן בַּכְּלָל. וּכְתִיב (שם) אָנֹכִי אֶדְרֹשׁ מֵעִמּוֹ, בִּידֵי שָׁמַיִם:


מיתתן בידי שמים – as it is written (Deuteronomy 18:19): “And if anybody fails to heed the words he speaks [in My name]…,” we call him “he does not heed,” and we call him “he should not proclaim” and we call him, “he himself does not heed My words,” all three are included, and it is written (ibid.): “I myself will call him to account,” by the hands of Heaven.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:6:1)


**לִרְשׁוּת הַבַּעַל.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁמָּסַר הָאָב לִשְׁלוּחֵי הַבַּעַל וַעֲדַיִן הִיא בַדֶּרֶךְ, תּוּ לֹא קָרֵינָא בָהּ בֵּית אָבִיהָ:


לרשות הבעל – such as the father who handed her over to the agents of the husband and still is still on the way, furthermore, we don’t call refer to her as “the house of her father.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:6:2)


**לְאוֹתָהּ מִיתָה.** שֶׁהָיוּ מְחַיְּבִין אֶת הַנִּדּוֹן:


לאותה מיתה – that they make the judged liable for.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sanhedrin 11:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sanhedrin/r/11:6:3)


**וּבוֹעֲלָהּ.** כְּלוֹמַר, וְכָל הַבּוֹעֲלִים נִדּוֹנִים כְּמִיתַת הַנִּבְעֶלֶת, חוּץ מִבּוֹעֵל בַּת כֹּהֵן, שֶׁהִיא בִשְׂרֵפָה וְהוּא בְחֶנֶק:


ובועלה – that is to say, all of those who those [men] who have sexual intercourse are judged like the death of the one (i.e., the woman) who had sexual intercourse, except for the case of a person who has sexual intercourse with the daughter of a Kohen – as she is [punished] by burning [by drinking a molten-liquid] and he [is punished] by strangulation.
